# Cluster A — Shell + Engine rules (241 rows)

Cluster A of the 2026-09-21 code↔Figma gap audit. Source: `docs/editor-feature-inventory-2026-09-16.md` lines 39–753 (Editor Shell) and 5231–5961 (Engine rules + appendix). Every SHIPPED / LIMITED / FLAGGED-VIABLE row cites a handler `path:line` under `packages/editor/`. Locations use the v3 vocabulary (Rail · Drawer panel · Full-canvas view · Inspector · Topbar · Modal · Popover · Side sheet · Context menu · ⌘K · Inline · Canvas · Footer · Toast). ## A · Handler re-reads

- SH-24 — CTA is gated by `isFeatureEnabled("publish")` (`StudioHeader.tsx:220`); off-flag renders disabled with "Publishing isn't switched on…" (`lifecycle.ts:105`). Review verbs (Send for review / Open feedback) still open the Review panel regardless of the flag. Marked FLAGGED-VIABLE, not SHIPPED.
- SH-39 — "Plugins" row opens Settings with `initialScreen: "plugins"`, which `SettingsTab.tsx:322` remaps to "integrations". No Plugins screen exists; marked LIMITED.
- SH-46 — "Ask AI" menu row exists only when `viewMode.fourToolRail` (`StudioHeader.tsx:847`); prod resolves `railMode="figma"` (`editorViewMode.ts:69-74`) → UNREACHABLE.
- SH-63 — Issues row `onSelectElement` is wired to `setIssuesOpen(false)` with an explicit "v1 just closes" comment (`AquibraStudio.tsx:622-626`) → STUB, confirming the appendix.
- SH-64 — "Fix ›" requires `issue.autoFixHint`; `LintState.ts:34` says "Absent until a rule learns to describe its own fix"; no rule in `linter/DSLinter.ts` sets it → the fixing/fix-failed bands, Open Brand and Ignore once are UNREACHABLE (engine `applyAutoFix` works when fed a hint).
- SH-66 — Rail unsaved dot depends on `dirtyTabIds`, which only ever contains "settings" (`LeftSidebar.tsx:664`); Settings is not on the six-item rail → UNREACHABLE in prod.
- SH-84 — Footer "Structure" button renders only under `fourToolRail` (`StudioFooter.tsx:215`); popover is mounted but has no other opener → UNREACHABLE.
- SH-87 — PreviewOverlay has no Share control at all (`PreviewOverlay.tsx:19-20`); the prose's "drawn, not wired" is Figma-side. Marked DUPLICATE of SH-43.
- SH-88 — ⌘K refuses to open in view mode and while a modal is open (`StudioHeader.tsx:259-269`), matching prose.
- SH-90 — "Fit to view" palette row prints `Ctrl+0` (`CommandPalette.tsx:133-135`) while the footer flyout and `commands.ts:170` print ⌘1 → the palette row is the wrong one.
- SH-91 — Engine "Preview" command (`defaultCommands.ts:400`) opens a new tab with exported HTML but is deduped by label behind the shell's ⌘P Preview row (`CommandPalette.tsx:184-218`); the new-tab variant is unreachable from ⌘K.
- SH-103/104 — PublishHistory is mounted in HistoryTab regardless of `FEATURE_PUBLISH` and fetches from the backend → SHIPPED; rollback admin-scoped (`PublishHistory.tsx:139`).
- SH-115 — Import JSON is not rendered at all (`CMSRecordsModal.tsx:462-469`); STUB in the Figma sense only.
- SH-120 — Onboarding progress is mirrored to the server (`useOnboardingOrchestrator.ts:261`), confirming "follows you across devices".
- EN-04 — Legacy 991/575 media queries confirmed at `StyleEngine.ts:190-191`; breakpoint constants (1023/767) at `breakpoints.ts:24-33`.
- EN-06 — "Watch" exists in `Viewport.ts:28` and the palette; the canvas renders it as `mobile` (`Canvas.tsx:831`) → LIMITED, palette-only.
- EN-24 — `SHORTCUTS` map has no consumer beyond a barrel re-export (`constants/index.ts:23`) → UNREACHABLE.
- EN-25 — `handleKeyboardDrag` has no consumer outside `dragDrop/` → UNREACHABLE.
- EN-32 — `noteUnrecordedAction` callers are bindings (`BaseBindingManager.ts:113,132,141`) and file delete (`MediaCommandLayer.ts:124`); "applying brand tokens" and "page created mid-load" from the prose were not found as callers.
- EN-48 — `exportVersions` / `importVersions` have no editor caller → UNREACHABLE.
- EN-52 — Crash sentinel is written (`RecoveryManager.ts:95`) but no code reads it back (`readCrashSentinel`/`lastCrash` grep empty) → LIMITED.
- EN-65 — `validateElementTree` and `analyzeTree` (the "Maximum nesting depth (30)", "500 children", landmark and page-audit copy) have no caller outside `shared/utils/nesting/` → UNREACHABLE; only live drop validation (`dropValidation.ts`) and `canNestElement` run.
- EN-89 — Only image files are handled on drop (`useDropExecution.ts:231-233`); text/HTML/URL parsing in `dragData.ts` has no consumer → LIMITED.
- EN-94 — `GlobalStyleManager` is constructed (`Composer.ts:250`) but nothing in `src/editor` reads it → UNREACHABLE; the purple preset never reaches a user.
- EN-108 — `Transforms.ts` string/number/date/utility maps are only consumed via `attributeTransforms` (`TraitDataBinding.ts:25`); CMS binding UI exposes 5 transforms (`cms.ts:135`) → LIMITED.
- EN-113 — Colour-blindness and palette-scheme utilities have no consumer in `src/editor`; contrast + "Fix contrast" do (`ColorTokenList.tsx:501`) → LIMITED.

# Cluster B — Canvas + Canvas interactions (198 rows)

Source: `docs/editor-feature-inventory-2026-09-16.md` lines 754–1256 (Canvas) and 1257–1703 (Canvas interactions), appendix 5900–5961. Every SHIPPED / LIMITED / FLAGGED-VIABLE row cites the handler (`path:line`, relative to `packages/editor/`). Status vocabulary per BRIEF Q3. Locations per BRIEF Q14 (v3 vocabulary). Rows marked "pointer" exist because the inventory describes the same code under two headings; they carry the same status and point at the canonical row in *Duplicate of*.

Row-ID prefixes: `CV-` canvas surface · `CI-` canvas interactions. Cross-cluster prefixes referenced: SH (shell/topbar/⌘K), PG (pages/layers/templates), AD (add panel), AS (assets/CMS), IN (inspector), EN (engine).

## B · Handler re-reads

Rows where code was read to confirm or override the prose.

- **CV-05** — `Canvas.css:778-781` has hide rules for desktop/tablet/mobile only; no `wide` row → LIMITED, not SHIPPED.
- **CV-14** — no explicit "wide → desktop" mapping exists; the effect comes from `ProInspector.tsx:90-92` falling back to `"desktop"` when `isValidBreakpoint("wide")` is false (`BREAKPOINTS` has no wide key). Prose claim holds, mechanism is implicit.
- **CV-15** — prose: "Watch (no UI door — legacy)". Code: `defaultCommands.ts:511-514` registers "Watch view" and `CommandPalette.tsx:183-228` surfaces every registry command in ⌘K → there IS a door. Re-statused LIMITED.
- **CV-30** — prose hedge confirmed: `elementInfo.ts:252` reads `data-buildrick-cms-bound`; `useCMSPreview.ts:88` writes `data-cms-bound`. Glyph cannot appear → STUB.
- **CV-32** — `InspectorToggle` component has zero consumers (`grep -rn InspectorToggle src`): only `useInspectorMode` is imported (`Canvas.tsx:27,291`). UNREACHABLE confirmed.
- **CV-41** — `CanvasOverlayGroup.tsx:310-325` comment says the canvas-anchored align toolbar was REMOVED 2026-08-31, but `SelectionBoxOverlay.tsx:535-548` still renders `<AlignmentToolbar>` 48px above the combined box. Two toolbars existed; one survives. Marked DUPLICATE of the inspector row per the same comment's "One concept, one home".
- **CV-53** — compact label's parent tooltip says "(Alt+↑)"; `useCanvasKeyboard.ts:261` binds ⌥↑ to *reorder*, not select-parent. Copy bug noted.
- **CV-57** — `useCanvasSelectionBox` exported from `hooks/index.ts:56-60`, no other importer → UNREACHABLE.
- **CV-71** — guides persist via `localStorage` (`useCanvasGuides.ts:36-51`), not with the site → LIMITED.
- **CV-79** — prose said "⌘K command … not on the footer". Confirmed wiring: `defaultCommands.ts:450-453` (⌘⇧C) → `ui:toggle:component-view` → `useComposerInit.ts:444,455`; ⌘K lists it through the registry loop. SHIPPED.
- **CV-80 / CV-81** — `showOutlines` never passed by `StudioPanels.tsx:506-531`; `toggleDevMode`/`toggleOverlay("devMode")` have no callers and `devMode` is declared-but-unused in `CanvasOverlayGroup.tsx:92`. Both UNREACHABLE.
- **CV-93** — "Browse templates" row prints shortcut "T" (`useCanvasCommandPalette.ts:234`); nothing binds T. "Open page settings (SEO…)" emits `UI_PANEL_OPEN {panel:"pages"}` — opens Pages tab, not SEO (comment at :250-252). LIMITED.
- **CV-94** — collab row filtered at `useCanvasCommandPalette.ts:326` on `isFeatureEnabled("collab")`; handler swallows errors (`.catch(() => {})`) → FLAGGED-VIABLE, note on silent failure.
- **CV-99 / CI-85** — `RemoteCursorsOverlay` is mounted unconditionally (`CanvasOverlayGroup.tsx:390`) and not flag-checked; it only has data when a session exists, and every session door is flag-gated → FLAGGED-VIABLE, not SHIPPED.
- **CI-01** — no ⋯-to-context-menu route: the toolbar's ⋯ (`ToolbarActionsSection.tsx:90-190`) opens its own 4-item dropdown. Prose correct.
- **CI-24** — `standaloneActions.ts:44` hides the row when `openAI` is undefined; `useCanvasContextMenu.ts:109-116` only supplies it when the shell passed `onAIRequest` (`StudioPanels.tsx:525` does). SHIPPED.
- **CI-30** — `Canvas.tsx:636-651` calls `select(el)` on right-click before opening the menu; a non-primary member therefore collapses the selection. Prose correct → LIMITED.
- **CI-33** — `elementStack` computed (`Canvas.tsx:637-643`) and passed (`useCanvasContextMenu.ts:116`); no action in `menus/actions/*` reads it. UNREACHABLE.
- **CI-46** — Shift only changes the cursor (`useCursorIntelligence.ts`); `useCanvasElementDrag.ts:122,201` records `shift` but the drop path does not branch on it. STUB.
- **CI-54** — drop rule depth 30 (`dropValidation.ts`) vs `MAX_NESTING_DEPTH: 50` (`config.ts:141`) — two ceilings, noted.
- **CI-70** — `commands.ts:199` table entries have no `KeybindingManager` registration; the chords the prose lists as "not bound" (⌘' ⌘; ⌘R) ARE bound, but by `CanvasFooterToolbar.tsx:268-272`, so the table is stale rather than the keys dead.
- **CI-73** — Topbar `IconButton label="Comments"` (`Topbar.tsx:269`) takes only `pressed`; no count prop exists. Prose "no count badge" confirmed.
- **CI-83** — `StudioHeader.tsx:786` passes `max: 2`; `Presence.tsx:67` default is 3. Header wins → two faces then "+N". Prose said "up to 3".
- **CI-84** — header renders presence only when `collaborationState !== "disconnected"` (`StudioHeader.tsx:774`), mapping the rest to `live`/`reconnecting`; the `offline` pill copy exists in `Presence.tsx:45` but the header never sends it. Noted.
- **CI-92** — `InteractionRuntime.ts:330-331`: `reverseAnimation` body is "For now, just a placeholder." Listeners are bound (:172,:181,:196-197); effect is nil → STUB.

# Cluster C — Pages / Templates / Layers / History + Add (183 rows)

Cluster C of the 2026-09-21 code↔Figma feature-gap audit. Source of truth for "what exists": `docs/editor-feature-inventory-2026-09-16.md` lines 1704–2050 (left drawer tabs), 2051–2606 (Add / Insert · Element Picker · Components · DS catalogue · AI panel · element-type reference · known gaps) and the appendix 5900–5961. Every SHIPPED / LIMITED / FLAGGED-VIABLE row carries a `path:line` read in this session (all paths relative to `packages/editor/`). Prose that hedged ("old", "legacy", "unreachable", "no UI", "blank thumbnails", "duplicated") was re-read in code — see *Handler re-reads* at the end. Local reads only; no Figma, no browser, no `src/` edits.

Status vocabulary (BRIEF Q3): SHIPPED · LIMITED · FLAGGED-VIABLE · STUB · UNREACHABLE · DUPLICATE. Location vocabulary (Q14): Rail · Drawer panel · Full-canvas view · Inspector · Topbar · Modal · Popover · Side sheet · Context menu · ⌘K · Inline · Canvas · Footer · Toast.

Row IDs: `PG-01…PG-113` (Pages · Templates · Layers · History · shared drawer pieces), `AD-01…AD-70` (Add panel · Element Picker modal · Components · DS catalogue · AI panel · engine element types · known-gaps index).

## C · Handler re-reads

Rows where the prose hedged or the status depended on code, and what the code says (one line each).

- **PG-01 / PG-70 / PG-86 / PG-106 — "help ?" in panel headers.** `LeftSidebar.tsx:626-630` builds `commonTabProps` without `onHelpClick`; `chrome-ui/PanelHeader.tsx:75-76` renders Help only when the handler exists → no drawer panel shows a "?" today. Inventory drift.
- **PG-10 — status chips.** `PagesTab.css:291-310` has `.bd-pg-chip.live/draft/scheduled/hidden/password`; `PageRow.tsx` never emits a chip element (only the home / external glyph at :233-247 and `getStatusLabel` into the aria-label at :139-149). Confirmed "chip styles exist but unused". No UI creates an "external" page.
- **PG-15 — Copy link.** `usePages.ts:341-354`: with no `domain` in project metadata the action is an info toast and no link is copied — works, gated on a domain.
- **PG-24 / PG-25 — folders "browser-only".** `useFolders.ts:5,35-58` reads/writes `localStorage` keyed by site id; nothing reaches the project or the deploy → LIMITED.
- **PG-30 — autosave timing.** `PageSettingsDrawer.tsx:60` is the 500 ms `setTimeout`; toasts and blocking validation at `usePageSettings.ts:245-306`.
- **PG-34 — "Write with AI".** `SeoTab.tsx:93-107` calls `generateContent(prompt, "headline", "professional")` from `shared/utils/openai.ts:92-105`, which goes through `aiTrpcClient` (server), not a client-side key.
- **PG-39 / PG-40 — Hidden / Password.** `AdvancedTab.tsx:47-58` prints the deploy exclusion; `usePages.ts:116-127` documents that unset visibility ships as live. Password is never enforced on a published site → LIMITED, copy is honest.
- **PG-45 — "Templates … also reached from Insert's TEMPLATES group".** `build/catalog/groups.ts:2-3` ("TEMPLATES is OUT of Insert"); `buildInsertGroups` returns only elements / blocks / components / mine → stale prose.
- **PG-47 — Sections.** Every `SITE_TEMPLATES` entry has `pageCount` (templatesData.ts:186-298) → `DrawerGallery.tsx:101` `sections` is always `[]`; `useTemplateSelection.ts:69-77` "sections" filter matches nothing → STUB.
- **PG-46 / PG-65 — saved templates "in the compact gallery".** `DrawerGallery.tsx:100-101` filters `SITE_TEMPLATES` only; `getMyTemplates()` is read solely by `useTemplateSelection.ts:65-69` (full gallery "My Templates" pill). Compact drawer never lists them.
- **PG-66 — applying a saved template.** `TemplatesTab.tsx:117-119` resolves `detailTemplate` from `SITE_TEMPLATES` only; `handleApplyToCurrent` / `handleAddAsNewPage` (:173-197) also `SITE_TEMPLATES.find`. A My-Templates card click (`:553`) sets `detailId`, `detailTemplate` becomes `null`, no detail renders, the width effect (:135-147) still widens the panel → STUB.
- **PG-64 — Save page as template door.** Only emitter of `TEMPLATE_SAVE_REQUESTED` is the ⌘K command `useCanvasCommandPalette.ts:173-180`; listener `useEditorEventListeners.ts:100-108` opens `SaveTemplate` via `StudioModals.tsx:12,168`. Server mirror at `useStudioHandlers.ts:144` → `templateSync.ts:101`.
- **PG-67 / PG-68 — legacy My Templates list and old preview modal.** `src/templates/MyTemplates.tsx` and `TemplatePreview.tsx` are exported from `src/templates/index.ts` and imported nowhere else (grep over `src/**/*.ts{,x}` excluding tests). UNREACHABLE confirmed.
- **PG-69 — engine TemplateManager.** `src/engine/templates/TemplateManager.ts:1-11` carries `@deprecated DEAD/SIMULATED`, superseded by `importHTMLToActivePage`.
- **PG-57 — "older 3-button device selector (used only inside template preview)".** `TemplatePreviewModal.tsx:84-90` defines its own Desktop / Tablet / Mobile toggle; it is live inside the preview. The appendix item is about the canvas selector (cluster CV), not this.
- **PG-60 — auto-save on template apply.** `shared/types/versions.ts:88` `autoCheckpointEvents: ["template:applied"]`; `VersionTimelineManager.ts:138-150` subscribes and calls `autoCheckpoint`; dedupe at :197-243.
- **PG-79 / PG-80 / PG-81 — layer dims / locks / names "remembered per page in the browser".** `layersPersistence.ts:25-75` is `localStorage` per page id; `useLayerActions.ts:109-125` persists all three sets → LIMITED (device-local).
- **PG-82 — top-level rows cannot be dragged.** `LayerTreeItem.tsx:99` `canDrag = composer && layer.depth > 0 && !isLocked`.
- **PG-84 — older icon-style layer context menu.** `src/editor/sidebar/tabs/layers/components/LayerContextMenu.tsx` (Lucide icons, Rename / Group / Lock / Delete) has no importer; `panels/layers/index.tsx:13` imports `./components/LayerContextMenu` (the board 1082:4527 menu). UNREACHABLE confirmed.
- **PG-83 — "Copy link" in the layer menu.** `useLayerContextActions.ts:28-43` builds an editor URL with the element id and copies it (toasts on success / failure) — live, not a placeholder.
- **PG-91 / PG-93 — versions "persist across sessions".** `VersionTimelineManager.ts:84-133` + `engine/storage/VersionHistoryStorage.ts` are IndexedDB; no server call. Persist across reloads on one browser only → LIMITED.
- **PG-92 — thumbnails.** `VersionTimelineManager.ts:237` `visualSnapshot: null` for auto-checkpoints; only `createVersion` captures one → hover thumbnail exists for named saves only.
- **PG-97 / PG-98 — AI in history.** `useAISummary.ts:109-127` POSTs `/api/trpc/ai.summarize`; `useAutoMilestone.ts:150-205` POSTs `/api/trpc/ai.milestoneSuggest`, gated on `versions.isAvailable()` and a 30 s cooldown, failing silently. Neither reads a feature flag → SHIPPED with a server-key dependency.
- **PG-104 — Published view.** `HistoryTab.tsx:408-420` mounts `PublishHistory` (`shell/PublishHistory.tsx:136`) — the same component the Publish panel embeds → DUPLICATE-of pointer to cluster ST kept, row status SHIPPED because the History door is its canonical home per the code comment (:406-407).
- **PG-110–PG-113 — four shared drawer pieces.** `ViewSwitcher`, `FeatureCard`, `FilterChips`, `StickyFooter` are referenced only by `sidebar/shared/index.ts` (and `headerIcons.tsx` for ViewSwitcher). No screen mounts them → UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-02 — "Replace with block…".** `standaloneActions.ts:24-35` emits `UI_INSERT_OPEN_GROUP {group: "blocks"}`; `BuildTab.tsx:54-62` opens the group. Live.
- **AD-07 — only ELEMENTS draggable.** `GroupSection.tsx:12-18` passes `draggable` for element rows only; block cards (:60-82) and mine rows (:48-58) have no drag handlers; `SearchResults.tsx:76-80` same rule.
- **AD-08 — "Soon" rows.** `GroupSection.tsx:102-131,177-190` implement the disabled row; `catalog.ts` sets `disabled` on no entry → UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-09 — blank block thumbnails.** `GroupSection.tsx:262-276` renders `b.preview` when set, else a blank `bg-subtle` box; `grep -rn "preview:" src/blocks` → 0 hits → every card is the fallback. `shared/types/block.ts:27` declares the optional field.
- **AD-11 — MINE not searched / no empty copy.** `search.ts:21-50` takes elements, blocks, components only; `GroupSection.tsx:48-58` maps `mine` rows with no empty branch.
- **AD-13 — "⌥ Paste HTML…" shortcut.** `BuildTab.tsx:101-117,249` — click handler only; no `altKey` listener anywhere in `build/`.
- **AD-15 — "bring them back from Help".** `TipsFooter.tsx:131` title only; `useBuildTab.ts:170-176` has `dismissTip` and no un-dismiss; Help itself is never wired (PG-106) → STUB.
- **AD-17 — loading / error states.** `InsertStateBlocks.tsx:55-93` exports `InsertLoadingSkeleton` / `InsertLoadError`; zero importers outside the file → UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-19 — media insert opens Assets.** `useBlockInsertion.ts:144-152` emits `ui:switch-tab {assets}` then `element:needs-asset` after 100 ms for image / video / audio / icon / svg / lottie with no `src`.
- **AD-21 / AD-22 — Element Picker modes.** `UnifiedSelectionToolbar.tsx:322-326` is the only mount and passes `position: "child"`; `BlockPickerModal.tsx:131-135` holds the three titles → before / after UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-23 — nesting refused silently.** `BlockPickerModal.tsx:91-99`: `devLogger` + `onClose()`, no toast.
- **AD-24 / AD-25 — Recents / Favorites only in the modal.** `ElementsTab.tsx` (the picker library) owns them (:92-116, :128-183); `BuildTab.tsx` has no recents / favourites state → appendix line confirmed.
- **AD-27 — "Typography" never appears.** `elements/constants.ts:85-104`: only `Other: "Typography"` maps there and no block carries category "Other" (inventory) → category list omits it at runtime.
- **AD-30 / AD-33 — components "stored in the browser".** `ComponentStorage.ts:32,66-70` = IndexedDB; `ComponentManager.ts:90` `maxComponents: 100` → LIMITED.
- **AD-31 — export / import.** `ComponentManager.ts:496-510` implements both; no call from `src/editor/**` → UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-32 — ⌘K "Open Components panel".** Not present in `useCanvasCommandPalette.ts:88-320` (commands listed there: Undo … Search stock photos). ⇧A works via `useSidebarKeyboard.ts:37-40`.
- **AD-34 — filters / favourites.** `useComponentsState.ts:79-131,167-212` keep the state; `ComponentsTab.tsx:234-237` states "no search, no filter chips" and renders none → UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-36 — "No Preview" always.** `ComponentDetailScreen.tsx:281-290` branches on `component.thumbnail`; the only writer in `ComponentManager.ts:368` copies an existing value on duplicate; nothing generates one.
- **AD-42 — variant chips.** `ComponentDetailScreen.tsx:105-130` keeps `selectedVariants` in local state; `handleVariantChange` never touches `composer` → STUB.
- **AD-43 — Create Component doors.** Panel: `LeftSidebar.tsx:548-566` (toast when nothing selected, else `COMPONENT_CREATE_REQUESTED`); canvas: `standaloneActions.ts:71-80` "Save as component"; listener `useEditorEventListeners.ts:6` → `useDomainModals.ts:87-92`.
- **AD-44 — variant values after creation.** `CreateComponentModal.tsx:253` hint; no variant-value editor exists in `component-library/` → STUB.
- **AD-47 / AD-48 / AD-49 — Rename, Select Variant, legacy row.** `handleRename` (`useComponentsState.ts:308-316`) and `handleSwapVariant` (:338-400) have no caller; the modals in `ComponentsTab.tsx:322-395` render only when those setters fire; `ComponentRow.tsx` is barrel-exported only → all UNREACHABLE.
- **AD-51 — Generate with AI.** `ComponentsSection.tsx:64-70` phrases the flag; the row is blocked with the tooltip when `FEATURE_DS_AI` is off → FLAGGED-VIABLE per BRIEF Q3.
- **AD-52 — draggable catalogue grid.** `CatalogSection.tsx` / `CatalogCard.tsx` imported by no screen (`ComponentsSection.tsx:21-26` imports `CATALOG` data only) → UNREACHABLE; AI insert path (`applySetStyle.ts:440,610`) still reaches `placeCatalogComponent`.
- **AD-53 — AI entry points.** `useEditorShortcuts.ts:168-172` (⌘J) and `ProInspector.tsx:404` emit `ui:switch-tab {ai}` → `StudioPanels.tsx:340-342` `setAiInInspector(true)`; but shortcut **I** goes through `useSidebarKeyboard.ts:43-47` → `onTabChange("ai")` → `TabRouter.tsx:156` renders `AITab` in the LEFT drawer. The drawer placement is live, contrary to the prose. No ⌘K "AI" command found.
- **AD-56 / AD-62 — AI backend.** `useStreamPrompt.ts:114` subscribes to `client.ai.streamPrompt` (tRPC subscription); `RECONNECT_BUDGET = 2` at :83; error kinds map PRECONDITION_FAILED / TOO_MANY_REQUESTS to the two state blocks (`AITab.tsx:269-303`). No editor feature flag in `sidebar/tabs/ai/`.
- **AD-63 — publish from chat.** `useAiActionGate.ts:30-110` reads no feature flag; it needs `?siteId=` and the Vercel-backed publish pipeline → classed with the Publish family (FLAGGED-VIABLE) rather than SHIPPED, because without `FEATURE_PUBLISH` product-wise there is no publish to propose.
- **AD-65 — "Improve with AI" hidden when unwired.** `standaloneActions.ts:44-45` `isVisible: ({openAI}) => Boolean(openAI)`.
- **PG-103 — Esc exits time-travel.** `TimeTravelScrubber.tsx:293` binds Escape (and Ctrl+Shift+T); `HistoryTab.tsx:357-366` prints "Esc exits" only because it holds.
- **PG-100 — undo cap.** `HistoryManager.ts:82` `maxHistory: THRESHOLDS.HISTORY_MAX_SIZE` = 100 (`shared/constants/config.ts:108`); prune loop at :614.

## C · Unverified

Rows whose status could not be settled from code alone, and why.

- **PG-51 (tag chips).** `SUB_CATEGORY_TAGS` filter on `t.subCategory`; I did not confirm whether any built-in template sets `subCategory` (templatesData.ts read for `status`/`pageCount` only). If none do, every tag chip narrows to "No templates in this category" — a LIMITED note at best, not a status change.
- **PG-89 (approval band).** Depends on `reviews.status` from the Review domain (cluster ST/SH); I read the band's render conditions, not the review service. Marked SHIPPED on the strength of `SavesChrome.tsx:80-143`.
- **PG-104 (Published view / rollback).** `PublishHistory.tsx` was located (`:136`) but not read past its signature; rollback gating and states come from the inventory prose. Status SHIPPED inherited from the Publish family — cluster ST should confirm.
- **AD-28 (drag from the Element Picker modal).** The card is draggable and the canvas drop path exists, but whether an HTML5 drag from inside a Radix modal over its overlay actually lands on the canvas was not checked in a live app.
- **AD-32 / AD-53 (⌘K commands "Open Components panel" and "AI").** Neither string exists in `useCanvasCommandPalette.ts`. There may be a second command registry (the ⌘⇧P palette mentioned in the appendix) that I did not locate; the ⇧A and I shortcuts are verified doors, so the panels are reachable regardless.
- **AD-63 (publish from chat) status.** Classed FLAGGED-VIABLE by association with the Publish family; the action gate itself reads no flag. Owner call if it should instead be SHIPPED-with-dependency.
- **AD-69 (purple defaults).** Hex lines cited from a grep (`667eea` / `8b5cf6` across `src/blocks/`); the builders were not opened beyond those lines. `GlobalStyleManager.ts:71,91` also carries `#667eea` (cluster BR/EN).
- **PG-42 (custom head code plan gate).** Appendix lists "Custom code" as Pro-gated in Settings; the per-page field in `AdvancedTab.tsx` has no gate in code. Which one the owner intends is a product question.
- **PG-38 (OG image via URL only).** No Assets picker on the Social tab was found; a picker could exist in a shared field I did not open.

- AS-46 — `FolderTree.tsx:315` calls `deleteFolder(folder.id)` with no `force`; hook throws `FOLDER_NOT_EMPTY` (`useLibraryState.ts:289-299`); rejection unhandled → silent. Prose confirmed; `inspectFolder` prompt was designed but never wired.
- AS-48 — `LibraryManager.tsx:865` toasts "Trash coming soon". Prose confirmed.
- AS-69 — `MediaContextMenu.tsx:221` renders "Replace across pages…" only when `onReplaceAcross` is passed; `LibraryManager.tsx:1048-1059` never passes it → UNREACHABLE (prose said "not wired").

- AS-82 — Wizard "Content type" value has no consumer beyond the select. Prose confirmed (cosmetic).

# Cluster E — Inspector + Brand (181 rows)

Source: `docs/editor-feature-inventory-2026-09-16.md` lines 3593–4158 (Inspector) and 4159–4573 (Brand), appendix 5900–5961. Every SHIPPED / LIMITED / FLAGGED-VIABLE row carries a `path:line` handler citation read in this session (local reads only, no Figma, no browser, no `src/` edits). Status vocabulary per BRIEF Q3.

Convention used for three capabilities the prose says do **not exist at all** (no UI drawn, no code): they are listed with `STUB` and `NOT BUILT` in Notes so the gap matrix can match a Figma control against them (IN-101, IN-102, IN-107). Everything else uses the vocabulary literally.

## E · Handler re-reads

Every row where code was read to confirm or override the prose (one line each; "→" = what the code says).

- IN-06 AI-run takeover → real: `composer.on("ai:agent-run")` sets the card (ProInspector.tsx:143-149).
- IN-07 / IN-19 Whole site → `onWholeSite` flips `wholeSite`; scope + reach reset on selection change (ProInspector.tsx:112-116). Sub-line copy still says "Styles tab" (ScopeDropdown.tsx:170,178) — prose omitted the drift.
- IN-12 Bind popover → `cms.bindings.bindToField(elementId, collectionId, itemId, slug, "content")` (BindingPopover.tsx:161-167); unbind = `unbindAll` (:180); "+ Create Collection" calls the prop (:320).
- IN-14 Paste styles disabled → `disabled: !composer?.styleClipboard` (InspectorElementMenu.tsx:204). Duplicate wraps a transaction (:145-147).
- IN-16 / IN-17 Align / Distribute → `alignmentHandler.alignHorizontal / alignVertical / distribute` (MultiSelectToolbar.tsx:183-214); disabled thresholds 2 / 3 (:216-217).
- IN-18 Batch → one `beginTransaction("batch-multi-style")` around all ids (useBatchStyleHandler.ts:148-206).
- IN-24 Detach → Pro gate `useDSModeOptional()?.mode === "pro"` (DetachInstanceButton.tsx:56-57); real `components.detachInstance` (:91) with both toasts (:99,101).
- IN-26 Variant band → `components.updateInstanceVariant` (VariantSection.tsx:83) and `components.resetInstance` (:145); band now renders for instances without variants (:97-103).
- IN-27 Revert → wraps `revert-<prop>-<bp>` transaction (BreakpointOverrides.tsx:117).
- IN-28 Media row → image opens picker and writes `src` in a `media-source-change` txn (MediaSourceRow.tsx:68-74); video / SVG select the asset and emit `ui:switch-tab {tab:"assets", fullPage:true}` (:76-78).
- IN-31 Expand / collapse all → hook exports them (useInspectorSections.ts:4); only `panels/layers` consumes an unrelated `expandAll` — nothing in the inspector renders a control. UNREACHABLE confirmed.
- IN-33 Density → `getEditorViewMode().density` reads `?density=fewer` or `?view=readonly` (editorViewMode.ts:93); the doc block says nothing in the codebase sets it (:26-33). UNREACHABLE (URL-only).
- IN-49 Chain button → `TokenPickerPopover` list layout; unlink writes `resolveVar(value)` (SizeSection.tsx:92-99; FontControls.tsx:74,112).
- IN-51 Spacing box chip → `DSBindingChip state="token"` only when `isTokenVar(value)` (SpacingControls.tsx:73,101-106); there is no chain button on a side, so a side is only "bound" if a token var already sits in the value.
- IN-57 Manage site fonts → `composer.emit("ui:site-fonts")` (FontPicker.tsx:42).
- IN-59 Gradient → only Color 1 / Color 2 + angle are composed (BackgroundSection.tsx:142-222). LIMITED (2 stops) confirmed.
- IN-64 Schema Border → `USE_SCHEMA_BORDER` from localStorage `buildrick:schema-border` (featureFlags.ts:27; visual.tsx:36). UNREACHABLE dev flag confirmed.
- IN-72 Link → `href` / `target` / `rel` written via `setAttribute` in transactions (LinkSection.tsx:132-175); `#page:<id>` scheme (:191).
- IN-74 Interactions → `el.setInteractions` in a txn (effects.tsx:97-100); preview uses a CSS `bd-anim-*` animation (:114); `types.ts` holds 55 `label:` entries (44 presets + triggers/easings). No action type anywhere in `interactions/`. LIMITED confirmed.
- IN-75 Animation → `el.setAnimation` (effects.tsx:48); editor is `src/editor/animation/AnimationEditor.tsx`, whose only importer is `AnimationSection.tsx:12` — the "animation editor" is the inspector section.
- IN-81 Heading level → `el.setTagName(value)` (elementProperties/index.tsx:233-234).
- IN-89 Columns → creates / removes container children (handlers.ts:48-79).
- IN-93 Per-type settings → `propertiesRegistry.ts:944-1022` carries `uiOnly` specs for modal / accordion / slider; `grep uiOnly` outside that file returns nothing. UNREACHABLE (spec only) confirmed.
- IN-95 All CSS → `shouldRender: ctx.devMode === true` (element.tsx:74); `devMode = USE_DEV_MODE` from localStorage `buildrick:dev-mode` (ProInspector.tsx:98; featureFlags.ts:35). UNREACHABLE dev flag confirmed.
- IN-96 Number field → Enter / Escape handled (InputControls.tsx:205-209); no ArrowUp / ArrowDown branch anywhere in the file. LIMITED confirmed.
- IN-98 Eye toggle → `onClick` only calls `setHidden` (ColorInput.tsx:261-264); no `onChange`. STUB confirmed.
- IN-100 Custom tab → only rendered when `onCustomValue` is passed (TokenPickerPopover.tsx:101,196); ColorInput passes it (ColorInput.tsx:282), the chain buttons do not.
- IN-103 / IN-104 Binding chip → the only two consumers pass `state="token"` (ColorInput.tsx:154; SpacingControls.tsx:103). `off-ds` / `preset` unreachable confirmed.
- IN-105 Style writes → 300ms debounce (useStyleHandlers.ts:184,268-271); peers written inside the same transaction (:259-262).
- IN-109 Global style presets → `GlobalStyleManager.ts` has no importer under `src/editor`. UNREACHABLE confirmed.

LIMITED, prose's "disabled with tooltip" is wrong for shipped behaviour.

- IN-06 (AI-run takeover) — the card is verified, but whether any shipping AI path actually emits `ai:agent-run` was not traced (AI panel is cluster AD). If nothing emits it, downgrade to UNREACHABLE.
- IN-36 (section order) — profile table cited by file, not by line; the seven orders in the prose were not re-derived from `elementProfiles.ts`.
- IN-74 (Interactions runtime) — the prose says "runs on the GSAP runtime"; only the inspector preview (CSS `bd-anim-*`) was read, not `src/engine/interactions/InteractionManager.ts` or the export path. Status LIMITED stands on the inspector side alone.
- IN-105 / EN overlap — the desktop-first cascade and per-state rules were read only from the inspector hook, not from the engine (cluster EN).
- BR-54 (8 lint rules) — rule list taken from the prose; `useDSLint.ts` and the engine lint rules were not read line by line. The "unresolved binding never runs" claim is the prose's, not re-read.
- BR-65 / BR-67 (CSS-var applier, browser cache) — cited by file; the cross-tab reload path in `projectTokens.ts` was not read.
- BR-70 (silent v1→v5) — only the header + version constants were read; individual migration bodies not verified.
- BR-71 (B15 legacy canvas tokens) — status DUPLICATE is a judgement (same job as the chrome token set); the kit range says "legacy they replace" but no code marks it deprecated.
- IN-101 / IN-102 / IN-107 — carry STUB by the convention stated at the top (NOT BUILT); the vocabulary has no exact word for "absent".

## E · Cross-cluster duplicates suspected

- IN-14 Element actions (Duplicate / Copy styles / Paste styles / Delete) ↔ CI canvas context menu — same style clipboard (`composer.styleClipboard`) and same commands.
- IN-16 / IN-17 Align / Distribute ↔ CV canvas multi-select toolbar / keyboard align — `alignmentHandler` is one engine object.
- IN-21 Breakpoint pill ↔ CV canvas device switcher / topbar device buttons — both call `onBreakpointChange`; two controls for one state.
- IN-11 / IN-02 "✦ AI" doors ↔ AD AI panel / ⌘⇧P — three inspector doors into the same tab.
- IN-12 Bind to collection ↔ AS / Content CMS records modal — the only binding UI is here, but collection creation lives in Content.
- IN-28 Media source row ↔ IN-80 / IN-83 URL fields ↔ AS Asset library — three doors to the same `src` attribute.
- IN-53 inspector font picker ↔ BR-40 Brand font family picker — one `FontPickerDropdown`, two triggers (28px vs 32px).
- IN-57 Manage site fonts ↔ AS Site fonts dialog.
- IN-75 Animation section ↔ ST "animation editor" — `src/editor/animation/AnimationEditor.tsx` is only mounted inside `AnimationSection`; if ST lists it as its own feature, it is this row.
- IN-74 Interactions ↔ IN-75 Animation — overlapping preset lists (Fade / Slide / Bounce…), two ways to animate one element.
- IN-94 CSS classes ↔ BR-46 Classes — per-element edit vs site-wide read-only list of the same class names.
- IN-99 Token picker ↔ BR-16 / BR-18 — the picker lists the same tokens the Brand kind screens edit; the picker's empty hint still says "Design tab".
- IN-100 inspector Custom colour entry ↔ BR-39 Brand inline colour picker — two different colour pickers (one hex-text, one canvas + alpha).
- IN-107 colour mode (absent) ↔ BR-51 Brand Light / Dark ↔ SH topbar (prose says "or top bar"; no topbar consumer of `ColorModeToggle` found).
- IN-108 / BR-04 Beginner–Pro — one localStorage mode, controls in Brand, effects in Inspector.
- IN-109 engine global style presets ↔ BR-41 Brand Presets — two preset systems; only Brand's is surfaced.
- BR-47 Component styles list ↔ AD Components panel — read-only mirror of the same catalog.
- BR-25 Tokens › typography Fonts ↔ BR-50 Typography destination ↔ IN-54 / IN-55 — three typography surfaces (edit tokens, read fonts in use, edit the element).
- BR-53 / BR-54 Lint ↔ SH Issues chip / Issues panel ↔ ST publish "issues open" confirm — one lint state, three surfaces.
- BR-64 Apply ↔ SH onboarding checklist "Set your brand".
- BR-01 entry ↔ SH rail / site menu; BR-02 expand ↔ SH drawer container (320↔700 is the drawer's, not Brand's).
- BR-71 B15 legacy canvas tokens ↔ CI / CV chrome tokens.
- Prompt's "Brand Simple" shape (starters strip · six swatches · inline picker · font rows · Save) has no single code surface: it maps onto BR-44 (starters), BR-12 (preview swatches, read-only), BR-25 / BR-40 (fonts), BR-35 / BR-39 (picker in token detail), BR-07 (Save). No inline swatch editing exists at the root — BR-12 is not a control.
